[702] [tool_result]  RESULT: 1	/*
2	 * solver.h — Backtracking resolution engine for Epilog.
3	 *
4	 * The solver executes a list of goals against a Database using SLD resolution.
5	 * It is depth-first and left-to-right, matching standard Prolog semantics.
6	 *
7	 * Execution model:
8	 *   - A ChoicePoint records the state at each point where multiple clauses
9	 *     could match, so backtracking can restore and try the next clause.
10	 *   - The goal stack holds remaining goals for the current branch.
11	 *   - No recursion: the main loop pops goals and pushes new ones iteratively.
12	 *
13	 * The solver calls a SolutionCallback for each solution found.  The callback
14	 * returns SOLVE_NEXT to request more solutions or SOLVE_STOP to halt search.
15	 *
16	 * JPL rules: no recursion, bounded stacks, no post-init malloc.
17	 */
18	
19	#ifndef EPILOG_SOLVER_H
20	#define EPILOG_SOLVER_H
21	
22	#include <stdint.h>
23	#include "terms.h"
24	#include "variables.h"
25	#include "unifier.h"
26	#include "database.h"
27	#include "atoms.h"
28	
29	#define GOAL_STACK_CAPACITY    512U
30	#define CHOICE_STACK_CAPACITY  256U
31	#define SOLVE_VAR_BLOCK       1024U  /* vars allocated per frame copy  */
32	
33	/* -------------------------------------------------------------------------
34	 * SolveSignal — callback return value.
35	 * ------------------------------------------------------------------------- */
36	
37	typedef enum {
38	    SOLVE_NEXT = 0,  /* continue search, yield more solutions */
39	    SOLVE_STOP = 1   /* halt search after this solution       */
40	} SolveSignal;
41	
42	/* -------------------------------------------------------------------------
43	 * SolverEnv — everything the solver needs, passed by pointer.
44	 * ------------------------------------------------------------------------- */
45	
46	typedef struct {
47	    TermArena   *arena;         /* query-scoped arena (working terms)      */
48	    TermArena   *clause_arena;  /* persistent arena (stored clause terms)  */
49	    VarRegistry *vars;          /* query-scoped variable registry          */
50	    VarRegistry *clause_vars;   /* persistent clause variable registry     */
51	    Trail        trail;
52	    AtomTable   *atoms;
53	    Database    *db;
54	} SolverEnv;
55	
56	/* -------------------------------------------------------------------------
57	 * SolutionCallback
58	 *
59	 * Called once per solution. env carries the current (bound) state.
60	 * goals_in is the original query goal array; goal_count its length.
61	 * Return SOLVE_NEXT or SOLVE_STOP.
62	 * ------------------------------------------------------------------------- */
63	
64	typedef SolveSignal (*SolutionCallback)(SolverEnv *env,
65	                                        const TermRef *goals_in,
66	                                        uint32_t goal_count,
67	                                        void *userdata);
68	
69	/* -------------------------------------------------------------------------
70	 * Public API
71	 * ------------------------------------------------------------------------- */
72	
73	/* solver_env_init — zero-initialise env. Returns 0 or -1. */
74	int solver_env_init(SolverEnv *env, TermArena *arena, TermArena *clause_arena,
75	                    VarRegistry *vars, VarRegistry *clause_vars,
76	                    AtomTable *atoms, Database *db);
77	
78	/*
79	 * solve — run goals[0..goal_count-1] against env->db.
80	 * Calls cb for each solution found.
81	 * Returns the number of solutions found (0 if none).
82	 */
83	uint32_t solve(SolverEnv *env, const TermRef *goals, uint32_t goal_count,
84	               SolutionCallback cb, void *userdata);

[711] [tool_result]  RESULT: 1	/*
2	 * atoms.c — Atom table implementation for the Epilog inference engine.
3	 *
4	 * Strategy: open-addressed hash table with linear probing.
5	 * All storage is pre-allocated in AtomTable (no malloc after init).
6	 *
7	 * JPL rules observed:
8	 *   Rule 1  — no goto, no setjmp/longjmp, no recursion.
9	 *   Rule 2  — every loop has a fixed upper bound (ATOM_TABLE_CAPACITY).
10	 *   Rule 3  — no dynamic memory allocation after atom_table_init().
11	 *   Rule 4  — no function exceeds ~60 lines.
12	 *   Rule 5  — every parameter validated; every return value is meaningful.
13	 *   Rule 6  — preprocessor used only for include and simple constants.
14	 *   Rule 7  — pointer depth ≤ 1 in all operations.
15	 *   Rule 8  — compiled with -Wall -Wextra -Wpedantic.
16	 */
17	
18	#include "../include/atoms.h"
19	
20	#include <assert.h>   /* assert()   */
21	#include <string.h>   /* memset, strncpy, strlen, strncmp */
22	#include <stdint.h>
23	
24	/* -------------------------------------------------------------------------
25	 * Internal helpers
26	 * ------------------------------------------------------------------------- */
27	
28	/* fnv1a_32 — FNV-1a 32-bit hash over a NUL-terminated string. */
29	static uint32_t fnv1a_32(const char *s)
30	{
31	    uint32_t hash = 2166136261U; /* FNV offset basis */
32	    uint32_t i;
33	
34	    assert(s != NULL);
35	
36	    for (i = 0U; i < ATOM_MAX_LEN + 1U && s[i] != '\0'; ++i) {
37	        hash ^= (uint32_t)(unsigned char)s[i];
38	        hash *= 16777619U; /* FNV prime */
39	    }
40	    return hash;
41	}
42	
43	/*
44	 * probe_slot — linear-probe search.
45	 * Returns the index of the matching slot or the first empty slot.
46	 * *found is set to 1 if the atom already exists, 0 otherwise.
47	 * Returns ATOM_TABLE_CAPACITY when the table is full with no match.
48	 */
49	static uint32_t probe_slot(const AtomTable *t, const char *spelling,
50	                            uint32_t hash, int *found)
51	{
52	    uint32_t idx  = hash % ATOM_TABLE_CAPACITY;
53	    uint32_t step = 0U;
54	
55	    assert(t       != NULL);
56	    assert(spelling != NULL);
57	    assert(found   != NULL);
58	
59	    *found = 0;
60	
61	    for (step = 0U; step < ATOM_TABLE_CAPACITY; ++step) {
62	        const Atom *slot = &t->slots[idx];
63	
64	        if (slot->id == ATOM_ID_INVALID) {
65	            return idx; /* empty — can insert here */
66	        }
67	
68	        if (slot->hash == hash &&
69	            strncmp(slot->spelling, spelling, ATOM_MAX_LEN) == 0) {
70	            *found = 1;
71	            return idx; /* existing match */
72	        }
73	
74	        idx = (idx + 1U) % ATOM_TABLE_CAPACITY;
75	    }
76	
77	    return ATOM_TABLE_CAPACITY; /* table full, no match */
78	}
79	
80	/* -------------------------------------------------------------------------
81	 * Public API
82	 * ------------------------------------------------------------------------- */
83	
84	int atom_table_init(AtomTable *t)
85	{
86	    if (t == NULL) {
87	        return -1;
88	    }
89	    memset(t, 0, sizeof(AtomTable));
90	    return 0;
91	}
92	
93	AtomId atom_intern(AtomTable *t, const char *spelling)
94	{
95	    uint32_t hash;
96	    uint32_t idx;
97	    int      found;
98	    size_t   len;
99	    Atom    *slot;
100	
101	    if (t == NULL || spelling == NULL) {
102	        return ATOM_ID_INVALID;
103	    }
104	
105	    len = strnlen(spelling, ATOM_MAX_LEN + 1U);
106	    if (len > ATOM_MAX_LEN) {
107	        return ATOM_ID_INVALID;
108	    }
109	
110	    hash = fnv1a_32(spelling);
111	    idx  = probe_slot(t, spelling, hash, &found);
112	
113	    if (idx == ATOM_TABLE_CAPACITY) {
114	        return ATOM_ID_INVALID; /* table full */
115	    }
116	
117	    if (found) {
118	        return t->slots[idx].id;
119	    }
120	
121	    /* Insert new atom. */
122	    assert(t->count < ATOM_TABLE_CAPACITY);
123	
124	    slot = &t->slots[idx];
125	    /* memcpy + explicit NUL — avoids MSVC strncpy deprecation warning. */
126	    memcpy(slot->spelling, spelling, len);
127	    slot->spelling[len] = '\0';
128	    slot->hash = hash;
129	    slot->id   = (AtomId)(t->count + ATOM_ID_MIN);
130	
131	    t->count++;
132	    return slot->id;
133	}
134	
135	AtomId atom_lookup(const AtomTable *t, const char *spelling)
136	{
137	    uint32_t hash;
138	    uint32_t idx;
139	    int      found;
140	    size_t   len;
141	
142	    if (t == NULL || spelling == NULL) {
143	        return ATOM_ID_INVALID;
144	    }
145	
146	    len = strnlen(spelling, ATOM_MAX_LEN + 1U);
147	    if (len > ATOM_MAX_LEN) {
148	        return ATOM_ID_INVALID;
149	    }
150	
151	    hash = fnv1a_32(spelling);
152	    idx  = probe_slot(t, spelling, hash, &found);
153	
154	    if (!found || idx == ATOM_TABLE_CAPACITY) {
155	        return ATOM_ID_INVALID;
156	    }
157	
158	    return t->slots[idx].id;
159	}
160	
161	const char *atom_spelling(const AtomTable *t, AtomId id)
162	{
163	    uint32_t slot_index;
164	    uint32_t i;
165	
166	    if (t == NULL || id == ATOM_ID_INVALID) {
167	        return NULL;
168	    }
169	
170	    /*
171	     * Linear scan to find the slot whose id matches.
172	     * This path is not performance-critical (used for display / debug).
173	     * Bounded by ATOM_TABLE_CAPACITY (JPL Rule 2).
174	     */
175	    for (i = 0U; i < ATOM_TABLE_CAPACITY; ++i) {
176	        if (t->slots[i].id == id) {
177	            return t->slots[i].spelling;
178	        }
179	    }
180	
181	    (void)slot_index; /* suppressed: unused after refactor */
182	    return NULL;
183	}
184	
185	uint32_t atom_count(const AtomTable *t)
186	{
187	    if (t == NULL) {
188	        return 0U;
189	    }
190	    return t->count;
191	}

2	 * variables.c — Logic variable registry for the Epilog inference engine.
3	 *
4	 * Variables are allocated per-query from a fixed-size VarRegistry.
5	 * Binding chains are walked with an explicit bounded loop (no recursion).
6	 * No heap allocation occurs after var_registry_init().
7	 *
8	 * JPL rules observed:
9	 *   Rule 1  — no goto, no setjmp/longjmp, no recursion.
10	 *   Rule 2  — every loop has a fixed upper bound (VAR_REGISTRY_CAPACITY).
11	 *   Rule 3  — no dynamic memory allocation after var_registry_init().
12	 *   Rule 4  — no function exceeds ~60 lines.
13	 *   Rule 5  — every parameter validated; every return value is meaningful.
14	 *   Rule 6  — preprocessor used only for include and simple constants.
15	 *   Rule 7  — pointer depth ≤ 1 in all operations.
16	 *   Rule 8  — compiled with -Wall -Wextra -Wpedantic.
17	 */

28	/*
29	 * entry_at — bounds-checked slot accessor.
30	 * Returns NULL when id is out of range; avoids pointer arithmetic elsewhere.
31	 */
32	static VarEntry *entry_at(VarRegistry *r, VarId id)
33	{
34	    uint32_t idx;
35	
36	    assert(r != NULL);
37	
38	    if (id == VAR_ID_INVALID || id < VAR_ID_MIN) {
39	        return NULL;
40	    }
41	
42	    idx = id - VAR_ID_MIN;
43	
44	    if (idx >= VAR_REGISTRY_CAPACITY || idx >= r->count) {
45	        return NULL;
46	    }
47	
48	    return &r->entries[idx];
49	}
50	
51	/* const variant for read-only callers */
52	static const VarEntry *entry_at_c(const VarRegistry *r, VarId id)
53	{
54	    uint32_t idx;
55	
56	    assert(r != NULL);
57	
58	    if (id == VAR_ID_INVALID || id < VAR_ID_MIN) {
59	        return NULL;
60	    }
61	
62	    idx = id - VAR_ID_MIN;
63	
64	    if (idx >= VAR_REGISTRY_CAPACITY || idx >= r->count) {
65	        return NULL;
66	    }
67	
68	    return &r->entries[idx];
69	}

84	VarId var_alloc(VarRegistry *r, const char *name)
85	{
86	    VarEntry *slot;
87	    size_t    len;
88	    VarId     new_id;
89	
90	    if (r == NULL || name == NULL) {
91	        return VAR_ID_INVALID;
92	    }
93	
94	    if (r->count >= VAR_REGISTRY_CAPACITY) {
95	        return VAR_ID_INVALID;
96	    }
97	
98	    len = strnlen(name, VAR_NAME_MAX_LEN + 1U);
99	    if (len > VAR_NAME_MAX_LEN) {
100	        return VAR_ID_INVALID;
101	    }
102	
103	    new_id = (VarId)(r->count + VAR_ID_MIN);
104	    slot   = &r->entries[r->count];
105	
106	    memset(slot, 0, sizeof(VarEntry));
107	    memcpy(slot->name, name, len);
108	    slot->name[len] = '\0';
109	    slot->tag       = TERM_UNBOUND;
110	    slot->id        = new_id;
111	
112	    r->count++;
113	    return new_id;
114	}

116	int var_bind_atom(VarRegistry *r, VarId id, AtomId atom_id)
117	{
118	    VarEntry *e;
119	
120	    if (r == NULL || atom_id == ATOM_ID_INVALID) {
121	        return -1;
122	    }
123	
124	    e = entry_at(r, id);
125	    if (e == NULL) {
126	        return -1;
127	    }
128	
129	    if (e->tag != TERM_UNBOUND) {
130	        return -1; /* already bound */
131	    }
132	
133	    e->tag         = TERM_ATOM;
134	    e->value.atom_id = atom_id;
135	    return 0;
136	}
137	
138	int var_bind_var(VarRegistry *r, VarId id, VarId other_id)
139	{
140	    VarEntry *e;
141	
142	    if (r == NULL) {
143	        return -1;
144	    }
145	
146	    /* Binding a variable to itself is a no-op, not an error. */
147	    if (id == other_id) {
148	        return 0;
149	    }
150	
151	    e = entry_at(r, id);
152	    if (e == NULL) {
153	        return -1;
154	    }
155	
156	    if (entry_at(r, other_id) == NULL) {
157	        return -1; /* other_id doesn't exist */
158	    }
159	
160	    if (e->tag != TERM_UNBOUND) {
161	        return -1; /* already bound */
162	    }
163	
164	    e->tag          = TERM_VAR;
165	    e->value.var_id = other_id;
166	    return 0;
167	}
168	
169	int var_bind_term(VarRegistry *r, VarId id, TermRef ref)
170	{
171	    VarEntry *e;
172	
173	    if (r == NULL || ref == 0U) { return -1; } /* 0 == TERM_REF_INVALID */
174	
175	    e = entry_at(r, id);
176	    if (e == NULL)             { return -1; }
177	    if (e->tag != TERM_UNBOUND){ return -1; }
178	
179	    e->tag            = TERM_TERMREF;
180	    e->value.term_ref = ref;
181	    return 0;
182	}
183	
184	WalkResult var_walk(const VarRegistry *r, VarId id)
185	{
186	    WalkResult     result;
187	    const VarEntry *e;
188	    VarId           current;
189	    uint32_t        steps;
190	
191	    result.tag            = TERM_UNBOUND;
192	    result.value.var_id   = VAR_ID_INVALID;
193	    result.root_id        = VAR_ID_INVALID;
194	
195	    if (r == NULL || id == VAR_ID_INVALID) {
196	        return result;
197	    }
198	
199	    current = id;
200	
201	    /*
202	     * Follow the binding chain. Bounded by VAR_REGISTRY_CAPACITY so the
203	     * loop is statically provable to terminate (JPL Rule 2).
204	     * No path compression — keeps pointer depth ≤ 1 (JPL Rule 7).
205	     */
206	    for (steps = 0U; steps < VAR_REGISTRY_CAPACITY; ++steps) {
207	        e = entry_at_c(r, current);
208	        if (e == NULL) {
209	            return result; /* dangling id — return invalid */
210	        }
211	
212	        if (e->tag != TERM_VAR) {
213	            /* Reached an atom binding or an unbound variable — done. */
214	            result.tag      = e->tag;
215	            result.value    = e->value;
216	            result.root_id  = current;
217	            return result;
218	        }
219	
220	        current = e->value.var_id;
221	    }
222	
223	    /* Should be unreachable: cycle would require more entries than capacity. */
224	    assert(0);
225	    return result;
226	}
227	
228	int var_reset(VarRegistry *r)
229	{
230	    if (r == NULL) {
231	        return -1;
232	    }
233	    memset(r, 0, sizeof(VarRegistry));
234	    return 0;
235	}

238	{
239	    if (r == NULL) {
240	        return 0U;
241	    }
242	    return r->count;
243	}

20	TermRef term_make_atom(TermArena *a, AtomId id)
21	{
22	    Term   *t;
23	    TermRef ref;
24	
25	    if (a == NULL || id == ATOM_ID_INVALID) {
26	        return TERM_REF_INVALID;
27	    }
28	    if (a->count >= TERM_ARENA_CAPACITY) {
29	        return TERM_REF_INVALID;
30	    }
31	
32	    ref = (TermRef)(a->count + 1U); /* 1-based; 0 == TERM_REF_INVALID */
33	    t   = &a->nodes[a->count];
34	    memset(t, 0, sizeof(Term));
35	    t->kind         = TERM_KIND_ATOM;
36	    t->data.atom_id = id;
37	    t->ref          = ref;
38	    a->count++;
39	    return ref;
40	}

42	TermRef term_make_var(TermArena *a, VarId id, const char *name)

66	        t->data.var.name[1] = '\0';
67	    }

69	    a->count++;

95	TermRef term_make_compound(TermArena *a, AtomId functor, uint8_t arity,
96	                            const TermRef *args)
97	{
98	    Term    *t;
99	    TermRef  ref;
100	    uint8_t  i;
101	
102	    if (a == NULL || functor == ATOM_ID_INVALID) {
103	        return TERM_REF_INVALID;
104	    }
105	    if (arity > TERM_MAX_ARITY) {
106	        return TERM_REF_INVALID;
107	    }
108	    if (arity > 0U && args == NULL) {
109	        return TERM_REF_INVALID;
110	    }
111	    if (a->count >= TERM_ARENA_CAPACITY) {
112	        return TERM_REF_INVALID;
113	    }
114	    if (a->args.count + (uint32_t)arity > ARG_POOL_CAPACITY) {
115	        return TERM_REF_INVALID;
116	    }
117	
118	    ref = (TermRef)(a->count + 1U);
119	    t   = &a->nodes[a->count];
120	    memset(t, 0, sizeof(Term));
121	    t->kind                  = TERM_KIND_COMPOUND;
122	    t->data.compound.functor   = functor;
123	    t->data.compound.arity     = arity;
124	    t->data.compound.arg_start = a->args.count;
125	    t->ref                   = ref;
126	
127	    for (i = 0U; i < arity; ++i) {
128	        assert(a->args.count < ARG_POOL_CAPACITY);
129	        a->args.slots[a->args.count] = args[i];
130	        a->args.count++;
131	    }
132	
133	    a->count++;
134	    return ref;
135	}
136	
137	const Term *term_get(const TermArena *a, TermRef ref)
138	{
139	    uint32_t idx;
140	
141	    if (a == NULL || ref == TERM_REF_INVALID) {
142	        return NULL;
143	    }
144	    idx = ref - 1U;
145	    if (idx >= a->count) {
146	        return NULL;
147	    }
148	    return &a->nodes[idx];
149	}
150	
151	TermRef term_arg(const TermArena *a, TermRef ref, uint8_t n)
152	{
153	    const Term *t;
154	    uint32_t    pool_idx;
155	
156	    t = term_get(a, ref);
157	    if (t == NULL || t->kind != TERM_KIND_COMPOUND) {
158	        return TERM_REF_INVALID;
159	    }
160	    if (n >= t->data.compound.arity) {
161	        return TERM_REF_INVALID;
162	    }
163	
164	    pool_idx = t->data.compound.arg_start + (uint32_t)n;
165	    assert(pool_idx < ARG_POOL_CAPACITY);
166	    return a->args.slots[pool_idx];
167	}
168	
169	TermKind term_kind(const TermArena *a, TermRef ref)
170	{
171	    const Term *t = term_get(a, ref);
172	    if (t == NULL) {
173	        return (TermKind)0;
174	    }
175	    return t->kind;
176	}

[717] [tool_result]  RESULT: 1	/*
2	 * lexer.c — Tokenizer implementation.
3	 *
4	 * Single-pass, no backtracking, no heap allocation.
5	 * JPL rules: no recursion, bounded loop (len of src), no post-init malloc.
6	 */
7	
8	#include "../include/lexer.h"
9	#include <string.h>
10	#include <assert.h>
11	#include <stdint.h>
12	
13	/* -------------------------------------------------------------------------
14	 * Character class helpers
15	 * ------------------------------------------------------------------------- */
16	
17	static int is_alpha(char c)  { return (c>='a'&&c<='z')||(c>='A'&&c<='Z'); }
18	static int is_digit(char c)  { return c>='0' && c<='9'; }
19	static int is_lower(char c)  { return c>='a' && c<='z'; }
20	static int is_upper(char c)  { return c>='A' && c<='Z'; }
21	static int is_alnum(char c)  { return is_alpha(c) || is_digit(c) || c=='_'; }
22	static int is_space(char c)  { return c==' '||c=='\t'||c=='\r'||c=='\n'; }
23	
24	static int is_op_char(char c)
25	{
26	    return c=='='||c=='\\'||c=='+'||c=='-'||c=='*'||c=='/'||
27	           c=='<'||c=='>'||c=='@'||c=='#'||c=='^'||c=='~';
28	}
29	
30	/* -------------------------------------------------------------------------
31	 * append_token — add one token to the list; returns 0 or -1 on overflow.
32	 * ------------------------------------------------------------------------- */
33	
34	static int append_token(TokenList *out, TokenKind kind,
35	                        const char *text, int32_t int_val, uint32_t line)
36	{
37	    Token *t;
38	
39	    assert(out != NULL);
40	
41	    if (out->count >= TOKEN_LIST_CAPACITY) {
42	        return -1;
43	    }
44	
45	    t = &out->items[out->count];
46	    t->kind    = kind;
47	    t->int_val = int_val;
48	    t->line    = line;
49	
50	    if (text != NULL) {
51	        size_t len = strnlen(text, TOKEN_TEXT_MAX);
52	        memcpy(t->text, text, len);
53	        t->text[len] = '\0';
54	    } else {
55	        t->text[0] = '\0';
56	    }
57	
58	    out->count++;
59	    return 0;
60	}
61	
62	/* -------------------------------------------------------------------------
63	 * lex
64	 * ------------------------------------------------------------------------- */
65	
66	int lex(const char *src, TokenList *out)
67	{
68	    uint32_t i;
69	    uint32_t len;
70	    uint32_t line;
71	    char     buf[TOKEN_TEXT_MAX + 1U];
72	    uint32_t buf_pos;
73	
74	    if (src == NULL || out == NULL) {
75	        return -1;
76	    }
77	
78	    memset(out, 0, sizeof(TokenList));
79	    len  = (uint32_t)strnlen(src, 1U << 20); /* 1 MB cap */
80	    line = 1U;
81	    i    = 0U;
82	
83	    while (i < len) {
84	        char c = src[i];
85	
86	        /* skip whitespace */
87	        if (is_space(c)) {
88	            if (c == '\n') { line++; }
89	            i++;
90	            continue;
91	        }
92	
93	        /* line comment */
94	        if (c == '%') {
95	            while (i < len && src[i] != '\n') { i++; }
96	            continue;
97	        }
98	
99	        /* block comment */
100	        if (c == '/' && i + 1U < len && src[i + 1U] == '*') {
101	            uint32_t depth = 1U;
102	            i += 2U;
103	            while (i + 1U < len && depth > 0U) {
104	                if (src[i] == '\n')                          { line++; }
105	                if (src[i] == '*' && src[i+1U] == '/')      { depth--; i += 2U; continue; }
106	                if (src[i] == '/' && src[i+1U] == '*')      { depth++; i += 2U; continue; }
107	                i++;
108	            }
109	            continue;
110	        }
111	
112	        /* atom: lowercase-initial or operator-sequence */
113	        if (is_lower(c)) {
114	            buf_pos = 0U;
115	            while (i < len && is_alnum(src[i]) && buf_pos < TOKEN_TEXT_MAX) {
116	                buf[buf_pos++] = src[i++];
117	            }
118	            buf[buf_pos] = '\0';
119	            if (append_token(out, TOK_ATOM, buf, 0, line) < 0) { return -1; }
120	            continue;
121	        }
122	
123	        /* variable: uppercase or underscore initial */
124	        if (is_upper(c) || c == '_') {
125	            buf_pos = 0U;
126	            while (i < len && is_alnum(src[i]) && buf_pos < TOKEN_TEXT_MAX) {
127	                buf[buf_pos++] = src[i++];
128	            }
129	            buf[buf_pos] = '\0';
130	            if (append_token(out, TOK_VAR, buf, 0, line) < 0) { return -1; }
131	            continue;
132	        }
133	
134	        /* integer */
135	        if (is_digit(c)) {
136	            int32_t val = 0;
137	            buf_pos = 0U;
138	            while (i < len && is_digit(src[i]) && buf_pos < TOKEN_TEXT_MAX) {
139	                val = val * 10 + (int32_t)(src[i] - '0');
140	                buf[buf_pos++] = src[i++];
141	            }
142	            buf[buf_pos] = '\0';
143	            if (append_token(out, TOK_INT, buf, val, line) < 0) { return -1; }
144	            continue;
145	        }
146	
147	        /* single-quoted atom */
148	        if (c == '\'') {
149	            buf_pos = 0U;
150	            i++; /* skip opening quote */
151	            while (i < len && src[i] != '\'' && buf_pos < TOKEN_TEXT_MAX) {
152	                if (src[i] == '\n') { line++; }
153	                buf[buf_pos++] = src[i++];
154	            }
155	            if (i < len) { i++; } /* skip closing quote */
156	            buf[buf_pos] = '\0';
157	            if (append_token(out, TOK_ATOM, buf, 0, line) < 0) { return -1; }
158	            continue;
159	        }
160	
161	        /* two-character tokens */
162	        if (c == ':' && i + 1U < len && src[i+1U] == '-') {
163	            if (append_token(out, TOK_NECK,  ":-", 0, line) < 0) { return -1; }
164	            i += 2U; continue;
165	        }
166	        if (c == '?' && i + 1U < len && src[i+1U] == '-') {
167	            if (append_token(out, TOK_QUERY, "?-", 0, line) < 0) { return -1; }
168	            i += 2U; continue;
169	        }
170	        if (c == '\\' && i + 1U < len && src[i+1U] == '=') {
171	            if (append_token(out, TOK_OP,    "\\=", 0, line) < 0) { return -1; }
172	            i += 2U; continue;
173	        }
174	
175	        /* operator sequences (=, etc.) */
176	        if (is_op_char(c)) {
177	            buf_pos = 0U;
178	            while (i < len && is_op_char(src[i]) && buf_pos < TOKEN_TEXT_MAX) {
179	                buf[buf_pos++] = src[i++];
180	            }
181	            buf[buf_pos] = '\0';
182	            if (append_token(out, TOK_OP, buf, 0, line) < 0) { return -1; }
183	            continue;
184	        }
185	
186	        /* single-character punctuation */
187	        switch (c) {
188	            case '(': append_token(out, TOK_LPAREN, "(", 0, line); break;
189	            case ')': append_token(out, TOK_RPAREN, ")", 0, line); break;
190	            case ',': append_token(out, TOK_COMMA,  ",", 0, line); break;
191	            case '.':
192	                /* '.' followed by whitespace or EOF is clause terminator */
193	                if (i + 1U >= len || is_space(src[i+1U])) {
194	                    append_token(out, TOK_DOT, ".", 0, line);
195	                } else {
196	                    append_token(out, TOK_OP, ".", 0, line);
197	                }
198	                break;
199	            case '|': append_token(out, TOK_PIPE,  "|", 0, line); break;
200	            case ';': append_token(out, TOK_SEMI,  ";", 0, line); break;
201	            case '!': append_token(out, TOK_CUT,   "!", 0, line); break;
202	            default: {
203	                char err[2] = { c, '\0' };
204	                append_token(out, TOK_ERROR, err, 0, line);
205	                return -1;
206	            }
207	        }
208	        i++;
209	    }
210	
211	    append_token(out, TOK_EOF, "", 0, line);
212	    return 0;
213	}
214	
215	const char *tok_kind_name(TokenKind k)
216	{
217	    switch (k) {
218	        case TOK_ATOM:   return "atom";
219	        case TOK_VAR:    return "variable";
220	        case TOK_INT:    return "integer";
221	        case TOK_LPAREN: return "'('";
222	        case TOK_RPAREN: return "')'";
223	        case TOK_COMMA:  return "','";
224	        case TOK_DOT:    return "'.'";
225	        case TOK_NECK:   return "':-'";
226	        case TOK_QUERY:  return "'?-'";
227	        case TOK_PIPE:   return "'|'";
228	        case TOK_SEMI:   return "';'";
229	        case TOK_CUT:    return "'!'";
230	        case TOK_OP:     return "operator";
231	        case TOK_EOF:    return "EOF";
232	        case TOK_ERROR:  return "error";
233	        default:         return "unknown";
234	    }
235	}

2	 * main.c — Entry point for the Epilog logic programming runtime.
3	 *
4	 * Usage:
5	 *   epilog [--verbose|-v] [file.pl ...]
6	 *
7	 * JPL rules: no recursion, bounded loops, no post-init malloc.
8	 */
9

11	#include "include/log.h"
12	#include <stdio.h>

14

18	{

20	    int              i;

35	        if (argv[i][0] == '-') { continue; }

37	        if (repl_load_file(&s, argv[i]) < 0) { return 1; }
38	    }

[720] [tool_result]  RESULT: 1	/*
2	 * parser.c — Clause and query parser.
3	 *
4	 * Iterative top-down parser (no recursion — JPL Rule 1).
5	 * Uses an explicit work stack for nested compound arguments.
6	 *
7	 * JPL rules: no recursion, bounded stacks/loops, no post-init malloc.
8	 */
9	
10	#include "../include/parser.h"
11	#include <string.h>
12	#include <assert.h>
13	#include <stdio.h>
14	
15	/* -------------------------------------------------------------------------
16	 * Internal helpers
17	 * ------------------------------------------------------------------------- */
18	
19	/* peek — return current token without advancing. */
20	static const Token *peek(const Parser *p)
21	{
22	    assert(p != NULL && p->tokens != NULL);
23	    if (p->pos >= p->tokens->count) {
24	        return &p->tokens->items[p->tokens->count - 1U]; /* EOF */
25	    }
26	    return &p->tokens->items[p->pos];
27	}
28	
29	/* advance — consume and return current token. */
30	static const Token *advance(Parser *p)
31	{
32	    const Token *t = peek(p);
33	    if (t->kind != TOK_EOF) { p->pos++; }
34	    return t;
35	}
36	
37	/* expect — consume token of expected kind; returns NULL and sets error otherwise. */
38	static const Token *expect(Parser *p, TokenKind kind)
39	{
40	    const Token *t = peek(p);
41	    if (t->kind != kind) {
42	        snprintf(p->error_msg, sizeof(p->error_msg),
43	                 "line %u: expected %s, got %s '%s'",
44	                 t->line, tok_kind_name(kind), tok_kind_name(t->kind), t->text);
45	        return NULL;
46	    }
47	    return advance(p);
48	}
49	
50	/* -------------------------------------------------------------------------
51	 * parse_term — parse a single term from the token stream.
52	 * Returns TERM_REF_INVALID on error.
53	 *
54	 * Handles: atom, variable, integer, compound(args), op infix (= \=).
55	 * Iterative: compound args parsed with a small local array, no stack.
56	 * Max arity bounded by TERM_MAX_ARITY.
57	 * ------------------------------------------------------------------------- */
58	
59	static TermRef parse_term(Parser *p)
60	{
61	    const Token *t;
62	    AtomId       aid;
63	    VarId        vid;
64	    TermRef      ref = TERM_REF_INVALID;
65	
66	    t = peek(p);
67	
68	    /* integer */
69	    if (t->kind == TOK_INT) {
70	        advance(p);
71	        ref = term_make_int(p->arena, t->int_val);
72	    }
73	
74	    /* variable */
75	    else if (t->kind == TOK_VAR) {
76	        advance(p);
77	        vid = VAR_ID_INVALID;
78	        {
79	            uint32_t k;
80	            for (k = 0U; k < p->vars->count; ++k) {
81	                if (strncmp(p->vars->entries[k].name, t->text,
82	                            VAR_NAME_MAX_LEN) == 0) {
83	                    vid = p->vars->entries[k].id;
84	                    break;
85	                }
86	            }
87	        }
88	        if (vid == VAR_ID_INVALID) {
89	            vid = var_alloc(p->vars, t->text);
90	        }
91	        if (vid == VAR_ID_INVALID) {
92	            snprintf(p->error_msg, sizeof(p->error_msg),
93	                     "line %u: variable registry full", t->line);
94	            return TERM_REF_INVALID;
95	        }
96	        ref = term_make_var(p->arena, vid, t->text);
97	    }
98	
99	    /* cut — treat as atom '!' */
100	    else if (t->kind == TOK_CUT) {
101	        advance(p);
102	        aid = atom_intern(p->atoms, "!");
103	        if (aid == ATOM_ID_INVALID) { return TERM_REF_INVALID; }
104	        ref = term_make_atom(p->arena, aid);
105	    }
106	
107	    /* atom or compound */
108	    else if (t->kind == TOK_ATOM || t->kind == TOK_OP) {
109	        const char *name = t->text;
110	        advance(p);
111	        aid = atom_intern(p->atoms, name);
112	        if (aid == ATOM_ID_INVALID) {
113	            snprintf(p->error_msg, sizeof(p->error_msg),
114	                     "line %u: atom table full", t->line);
115	            return TERM_REF_INVALID;
116	        }
117	
118	        if (peek(p)->kind == TOK_LPAREN) {
119	            TermRef args[TERM_MAX_ARITY];
120	            uint8_t arity = 0U;
121	            advance(p); /* consume '(' */
122	            if (peek(p)->kind != TOK_RPAREN) {
123	                uint32_t a;
124	                for (a = 0U; a < (uint32_t)TERM_MAX_ARITY; ++a) {
125	                    TermRef arg = parse_term(p);
126	                    if (arg == TERM_REF_INVALID) { return TERM_REF_INVALID; }
127	                    args[arity++] = arg;
128	                    if (peek(p)->kind != TOK_COMMA) { break; }
129	                    advance(p);
130	                }
131	            }
132	            if (expect(p, TOK_RPAREN) == NULL) { return TERM_REF_INVALID; }
133	            ref = term_make_compound(p->arena, aid, arity, args);
134	        } else {
135	            ref = term_make_atom(p->arena, aid);
136	        }
137	    }
138	
139	    else {
140	        snprintf(p->error_msg, sizeof(p->error_msg),
141	                 "line %u: unexpected token %s '%s'",
142	                 t->line, tok_kind_name(t->kind), t->text);
143	        return TERM_REF_INVALID;
144	    }
145	
146	    if (ref == TERM_REF_INVALID) { return TERM_REF_INVALID; }
147	
148	    /* infix operator check: = or \= following any primary term */
149	    {
150	        const Token *op = peek(p);
151	        if (op->kind == TOK_OP &&
152	            (strncmp(op->text, "=",  2) == 0 ||
153	             strncmp(op->text, "\\=", 3) == 0)) {
154	            char    op_name[TOKEN_TEXT_MAX + 1U];
155	            AtomId  op_aid;
156	            TermRef rhs;
157	            TermRef pair[2];
158	
159	            memcpy(op_name, op->text, TOKEN_TEXT_MAX);
160	            op_name[TOKEN_TEXT_MAX] = '\0';
161	            advance(p);
162	
163	            rhs = parse_term(p);
164	            if (rhs == TERM_REF_INVALID) { return TERM_REF_INVALID; }
165	
166	            op_aid = atom_intern(p->atoms, op_name);
167	            if (op_aid == ATOM_ID_INVALID) { return TERM_REF_INVALID; }
168	
169	            pair[0] = ref;
170	            pair[1] = rhs;
171	            ref = term_make_compound(p->arena, op_aid, 2U, pair);
172	        }
173	    }
174	
175	    return ref;
176	}
177	
178	/* parse_term_list — parse a comma-separated goal list into out[]. */
179	static ParseResult parse_term_list(Parser *p, TermRef *out, uint32_t cap,
180	                                   uint32_t *count)
181	{
182	    uint32_t n = 0U;
183	
184	    assert(p != NULL && out != NULL && count != NULL);
185	    *count = 0U;
186	
187	    for (n = 0U; n < cap; ++n) {
188	        TermRef ref = parse_term(p);
189	        if (ref == TERM_REF_INVALID) {
190	            return PARSE_ERR_SYNTAX;
191	        }
192	        out[n] = ref;
193	        (*count)++;
194	        if (peek(p)->kind != TOK_COMMA) { break; }
195	        advance(p); /* consume ',' */
196	    }
197	
198	    if (*count == cap && peek(p)->kind == TOK_COMMA) {
199	        return PARSE_ERR_OVERFLOW;
200	    }
201	    return PARSE_OK;
202	}
203	
204	/* -------------------------------------------------------------------------
205	 * Public API
206	 * ------------------------------------------------------------------------- */
207	
208	int parser_init(Parser *p, const TokenList *tokens, TermArena *arena,
209	                AtomTable *atoms, VarRegistry *vars)
210	{
211	    if (p == NULL || tokens == NULL || arena == NULL ||
212	        atoms == NULL || vars == NULL) {
213	        return -1;
214	    }
215	    memset(p, 0, sizeof(Parser));
216	    p->tokens = tokens;
217	    p->arena  = arena;
218	    p->atoms  = atoms;
219	    p->vars   = vars;
220	    p->pos    = 0U;
221	    return 0;
222	}
223	
224	ParseResult parser_next_clause(Parser *p, ParsedClause *out)
225	{
226	    TermRef head;
227	
228	    if (p == NULL || out == NULL) { return PARSE_ERR_ARGS; }
229	    memset(out, 0, sizeof(ParsedClause));
230	
231	    /* reset variables for this clause */
232	    var_reset(p->vars);
233	
234	    if (peek(p)->kind == TOK_EOF) { return PARSE_EOF; }
235	
236	    head = parse_term(p);
237	    if (head == TERM_REF_INVALID) { return PARSE_ERR_SYNTAX; }
238	    out->head = head;
239	
240	    if (peek(p)->kind == TOK_NECK) {
241	        advance(p); /* consume ':-' */
242	        ParseResult r = parse_term_list(p, out->body, PARSER_MAX_BODY_GOALS,
243	                                        &out->body_len);
244	        if (r != PARSE_OK) { return r; }
245	    }
246	
247	    if (expect(p, TOK_DOT) == NULL) { return PARSE_ERR_SYNTAX; }
248	    return PARSE_OK;
249	}
250	
251	ParseResult parser_query(Parser *p, ParsedQuery *out)
252	{
253	    if (p == NULL || out == NULL) { return PARSE_ERR_ARGS; }
254	    memset(out, 0, sizeof(ParsedQuery));
255	
256	    var_reset(p->vars);
257	
258	    if (expect(p, TOK_QUERY) == NULL) { return PARSE_ERR_SYNTAX; }
259	
260	    ParseResult r = parse_term_list(p, out->goals, PARSER_MAX_BODY_GOALS,
261	                                    &out->goal_count);
262	    if (r != PARSE_OK) { return r; }
263	
264	    if (expect(p, TOK_DOT) == NULL) { return PARSE_ERR_SYNTAX; }
265	    return PARSE_OK;
266	}

[722] [tool_result]  RESULT: 1	/*
2	 * database.c — Clause database implementation.
3	 *
4	 * Predicates are indexed by functor+arity. Each predicate owns a contiguous
5	 * run of StoredClause slots in Database.clauses[]. assert appends; retract
6	 * shifts remaining clauses left (simple, safe, bounded).
7	 *
8	 * JPL rules: no recursion, bounded loops, no post-init malloc.
9	 */
10	
11	#include "../include/database.h"
12	#include <string.h>
13	#include <assert.h>
14	
15	/* -------------------------------------------------------------------------
16	 * Internal: find or create a PredEntry for functor/arity.
17	 * ------------------------------------------------------------------------- */
18	
19	static PredEntry *find_pred(Database *db, AtomId functor, uint8_t arity)
20	{
21	    uint32_t i;
22	
23	    assert(db != NULL);
24	
25	    for (i = 0U; i < db->pred_count; ++i) {
26	        if (db->preds[i].functor == functor &&
27	            db->preds[i].arity   == arity) {
28	            return &db->preds[i];
29	        }
30	    }
31	    return NULL;
32	}
33	
34	static PredEntry *find_or_create_pred(Database *db, AtomId functor,
35	                                       uint8_t arity)
36	{
37	    PredEntry *e = find_pred(db, functor, arity);
38	    if (e != NULL) { return e; }
39	
40	    if (db->pred_count >= DB_MAX_PREDICATES) { return NULL; }
41	
42	    e = &db->preds[db->pred_count];
43	    memset(e, 0, sizeof(PredEntry));
44	    e->functor = functor;
45	    e->arity   = arity;
46	    e->first   = db->clause_count; /* will be filled by assert */
47	    e->count   = 0U;
48	    db->pred_count++;
49	    return e;
50	}
51	
52	/* -------------------------------------------------------------------------
53	 * Public API
54	 * ------------------------------------------------------------------------- */
55	
56	int db_init(Database *db)
57	{
58	    if (db == NULL) { return -1; }
59	    memset(db, 0, sizeof(Database));
60	    return 0;
61	}
62	
63	int db_assert_last(Database *db, const ParsedClause *clause, TermArena *arena)
64	{
65	    const Term *head_term;
66	    AtomId      functor;
67	    uint8_t     arity;
68	    PredEntry  *pred;
69	    StoredClause *sc;
70	    uint32_t    body_i;
71	    uint32_t    insert_pos;
72	    uint32_t    shift;
73	
74	    (void)arena; /* reserved for future deep-copy; terms already in arena */
75	
76	    if (db == NULL || clause == NULL) { return -1; }
77	    if (db->clause_count >= DB_MAX_CLAUSES) { return -1; }
78	
79	    head_term = term_get(arena, clause->head);
80	    if (head_term == NULL) { return -1; }
81	
82	    switch (head_term->kind) {
83	        case TERM_KIND_ATOM:
84	            functor = head_term->data.atom_id;
85	            arity   = 0U;
86	            break;
87	        case TERM_KIND_COMPOUND:
88	            functor = head_term->data.compound.functor;
89	            arity   = head_term->data.compound.arity;
90	            break;
91	        default:
92	            return -1; /* head must be atom or compound */
93	    }
94	
95	    pred = find_or_create_pred(db, functor, arity);
96	    if (pred == NULL) { return -1; }
97	
98	    /*
99	     * Insert position: just after the last clause of this predicate.
100	     * All subsequent clauses (other predicates) must be shifted right.
101	     */
102	    insert_pos = pred->first + pred->count;
103	
104	    /* shift clauses after insert_pos right by one */
105	    for (shift = db->clause_count; shift > insert_pos; --shift) {
106	        db->clauses[shift] = db->clauses[shift - 1U];
107	        db->clauses[shift].id = (ClauseId)(shift + 1U);
108	    }
109	
110	    /* fix up pred->first for all predicates whose clauses moved right */
111	    {
112	        uint32_t pi;
113	        for (pi = 0U; pi < db->pred_count; ++pi) {
114	            if (&db->preds[pi] != pred &&
115	                db->preds[pi].first >= insert_pos) {
116	                db->preds[pi].first++;
117	            }
118	        }
119	    }
120	
121	    sc = &db->clauses[insert_pos];
122	    memset(sc, 0, sizeof(StoredClause));
123	    sc->head     = clause->head;
124	    sc->body_len = clause->body_len;
125	    sc->id       = (ClauseId)(insert_pos + 1U);
126	
127	    assert(clause->body_len <= DB_MAX_BODY_GOALS);
128	    for (body_i = 0U; body_i < clause->body_len; ++body_i) {
129	        sc->body[body_i] = clause->body[body_i];
130	    }
131	
132	    pred->count++;
133	    db->clause_count++;
134	    return 0;
135	}
136	
137	int db_retract_first(Database *db, AtomId functor, uint8_t arity)
138	{
139	    PredEntry *pred;
140	    uint32_t   remove_pos;
141	    uint32_t   shift;
142	
143	    if (db == NULL || functor == ATOM_ID_INVALID) { return -1; }
144	
145	    pred = find_pred(db, functor, arity);
146	    if (pred == NULL || pred->count == 0U) { return -1; }
147	
148	    remove_pos = pred->first;
149	
150	    /* shift all subsequent clauses left */
151	    for (shift = remove_pos; shift + 1U < db->clause_count; ++shift) {
152	        db->clauses[shift] = db->clauses[shift + 1U];
153	        db->clauses[shift].id = (ClauseId)(shift + 1U);
154	    }
155	    db->clause_count--;
156	
157	    pred->count--;
158	    if (pred->count == 0U) {
159	        /* pred remains but is empty — leave it in place */
160	    }
161	
162	    /* fix up first for predicates whose run shifted left */
163	    {
164	        uint32_t pi;
165	        for (pi = 0U; pi < db->pred_count; ++pi) {
166	            if (&db->preds[pi] != pred &&
167	                db->preds[pi].first > remove_pos) {
168	                db->preds[pi].first--;
169	            }
170	        }
171	    }
172	
173	    return 0;
174	}
175	
176	const PredEntry *db_get_pred(const Database *db, AtomId functor, uint8_t arity)
177	{
178	    uint32_t i;
179	
180	    if (db == NULL || functor == ATOM_ID_INVALID) { return NULL; }
181	
182	    for (i = 0U; i < db->pred_count; ++i) {
183	        if (db->preds[i].functor == functor &&
184	            db->preds[i].arity   == arity) {
185	            return &db->preds[i];
186	        }
187	    }
188	    return NULL;
189	}
190	
191	const StoredClause *db_get_clause(const Database *db, uint32_t idx)
192	{
193	    if (db == NULL || idx >= db->clause_count) { return NULL; }
194	    return &db->clauses[idx];
195	}

[724] [tool_result]  RESULT: 1	/*
2	 * unifier.c — Robinson unification, iterative implementation.
3	 *
4	 * Uses an explicit work stack of (TermRef, TermRef) pairs to avoid recursion.
5	 * Bounded by UNIFY_STACK_CAPACITY (JPL Rule 2).
6	 *
7	 * JPL rules: no recursion, bounded stack, no post-init malloc.
8	 */
9	
10	#include "../include/unifier.h"
11	#include <string.h>
12	#include <assert.h>
13	
14	/* -------------------------------------------------------------------------
15	 * Trail
16	 * ------------------------------------------------------------------------- */
17	
18	int trail_init(Trail *t)
19	{
20	    if (t == NULL) { return -1; }
21	    memset(t, 0, sizeof(Trail));
22	    return 0;
23	}
24	
25	uint32_t trail_mark(const Trail *t)
26	{
27	    assert(t != NULL);
28	    return t->top;
29	}
30	
31	void trail_undo(Trail *t, VarRegistry *vars, uint32_t mark)
32	{
33	    assert(t != NULL && vars != NULL);
34	
35	    while (t->top > mark) {
36	        VarId    vid = t->bound[t->top - 1U];
37	        VarEntry *e;
38	        uint32_t  idx;
39	
40	        if (vid != VAR_ID_INVALID) {
41	            idx = vid - VAR_ID_MIN;
42	            if (idx < vars->count) {
43	                e = &vars->entries[idx];
44	                e->tag          = TERM_UNBOUND;
45	                e->value.var_id = VAR_ID_INVALID;
46	            }
47	        }
48	        t->top--;
49	    }
50	}
51	
52	/* -------------------------------------------------------------------------
53	 * deref — follow var→var chains to the root term.
54	 * Bounded by VAR_REGISTRY_CAPACITY (JPL Rule 2).
55	 * ------------------------------------------------------------------------- */
56	
57	TermRef deref(TermRef ref, const TermArena *arena, const VarRegistry *vars)
58	{
59	    uint32_t steps;
60	
61	    if (ref == TERM_REF_INVALID) { return TERM_REF_INVALID; }
62	
63	    for (steps = 0U; steps < VAR_REGISTRY_CAPACITY; ++steps) {
64	        const Term *t = term_get(arena, ref);
65	        if (t == NULL) { return TERM_REF_INVALID; }
66	
67	        if (t->kind != TERM_KIND_VAR) { return ref; }
68	
69	        /* variable: check if bound */
70	        {
71	            WalkResult wr = var_walk(vars, t->data.var.var_id);
72	            if (wr.tag == TERM_UNBOUND) { return ref; }           /* unbound var    */
73	            if (wr.tag == TERM_TERMREF) { ref = wr.value.term_ref; continue; } /* follow ref */
74	            if (wr.tag == TERM_VAR) {
75	                /* bound to another variable — find its TermRef in arena */
76	                uint32_t ni;
77	                for (ni = 0U; ni < arena->count; ++ni) {
78	                    const Term *candidate = &arena->nodes[ni];
79	                    if (candidate->kind == TERM_KIND_VAR &&
80	                        candidate->data.var.var_id == wr.root_id) {
81	                        ref = candidate->ref;
82	                        break;
83	                    }
84	                }
85	            }
86	        }
87	    }
88	    return ref;
89	}
90	
91	/* -------------------------------------------------------------------------
92	 * bind_var — bind a variable to a term, recording on the trail.
93	 * Returns 0 on success, -1 on overflow or already-bound.
94	 * ------------------------------------------------------------------------- */
95	
96	static int bind_var(VarId vid, TermRef target_ref, const TermArena *arena,
97	                    VarRegistry *vars, Trail *trail)
98	{
99	    const Term *target;
100	
101	    assert(vars != NULL && trail != NULL && arena != NULL);
102	
103	    if (trail->top >= TRAIL_CAPACITY) { return -1; }
104	
105	    target = term_get(arena, target_ref);
106	    if (target == NULL) { return -1; }
107	
108	    if (target->kind == TERM_KIND_VAR) {
109	        /* bind var-to-var so deref can follow the chain */
110	        if (var_bind_var(vars, vid, target->data.var.var_id) < 0) { return -1; }
111	    } else {
112	        /* atom, int, compound: bind to the TermRef so deref returns the term */
113	        if (var_bind_term(vars, vid, target_ref) < 0) { return -1; }
114	    }
115	
116	    trail->bound[trail->top] = vid;
117	    trail->top++;
118	    return 0;
119	}
120	
121	/* -------------------------------------------------------------------------
122	 * unify — iterative Robinson unification.
123	 * ------------------------------------------------------------------------- */
124	
125	typedef struct { TermRef a; TermRef b; } UnifyPair;
126	
127	int unify(TermRef a, TermRef b,
128	          const TermArena *arena, VarRegistry *vars, Trail *trail)
129	{
130	    UnifyPair stack[UNIFY_STACK_CAPACITY];
131	    uint32_t  top = 0U;
132	    uint32_t  steps;
133	
134	    if (arena == NULL || vars == NULL || trail == NULL) { return 0; }
135	
136	    stack[top].a = a;
137	    stack[top].b = b;
138	    top++;
139	
140	    for (steps = 0U; steps < UNIFY_STACK_CAPACITY && top > 0U; ++steps) {
141	        TermRef    ra, rb;
142	        const Term *ta, *tb;
143	
144	        top--;
145	        ra = deref(stack[top].a, arena, vars);
146	        rb = deref(stack[top].b, arena, vars);
147	
148	        if (ra == rb) { continue; } /* identical refs — trivially unified */
149	
150	        ta = term_get(arena, ra);
151	        tb = term_get(arena, rb);
152	
153	        if (ta == NULL || tb == NULL) { return 0; }
154	
155	        /* variable on left */
156	        if (ta->kind == TERM_KIND_VAR) {
157	            if (bind_var(ta->data.var.var_id, rb, arena, vars, trail) < 0) {
158	                return 0;
159	            }
160	            continue;
161	        }
162	
163	        /* variable on right */
164	        if (tb->kind == TERM_KIND_VAR) {
165	            if (bind_var(tb->data.var.var_id, ra, arena, vars, trail) < 0) {
166	                return 0;
167	            }
168	            continue;
169	        }
170	
171	        /* both atoms */
172	        if (ta->kind == TERM_KIND_ATOM && tb->kind == TERM_KIND_ATOM) {
173	            if (ta->data.atom_id != tb->data.atom_id) { return 0; }
174	            continue;
175	        }
176	
177	        /* both ints */
178	        if (ta->kind == TERM_KIND_INT && tb->kind == TERM_KIND_INT) {
179	            if (ta->data.int_val != tb->data.int_val) { return 0; }
180	            continue;
181	        }
182	
183	        /* both compounds */
184	        if (ta->kind == TERM_KIND_COMPOUND && tb->kind == TERM_KIND_COMPOUND) {
185	            uint8_t arity = ta->data.compound.arity;
186	            uint8_t i;
187	
188	            if (ta->data.compound.functor != tb->data.compound.functor) { return 0; }
189	            if (arity != tb->data.compound.arity) { return 0; }
190	
191	            if (top + (uint32_t)arity >= UNIFY_STACK_CAPACITY) { return 0; }
192	
193	            for (i = 0U; i < arity; ++i) {
194	                stack[top].a = term_arg(arena, ra, i);
195	                stack[top].b = term_arg(arena, rb, i);
196	                top++;
197	            }
198	            continue;
199	        }
200	
201	        /* kind mismatch */
202	        return 0;
203	    }
204	
205	    return (top == 0U) ? 1 : 0;
206	}

[726] [tool_result]  RESULT: 1	/*
2	 * builtins.c — Built-in predicate implementations.
3	 *
4	 * Each built-in is a static function matching the BuiltinFn signature.
5	 * builtin_lookup performs a linear scan over a fixed dispatch table.
6	 *
7	 * JPL rules: no recursion, bounded loops, no post-init malloc.
8	 */
9	
10	#include "../include/builtins.h"
11	#include "../include/parser.h"
12	#include <string.h>
13	#include <stdio.h>
14	#include <assert.h>
15	
16	/* -------------------------------------------------------------------------
17	 * term_write — recursive-free term printer using an explicit stack.
18	 * ------------------------------------------------------------------------- */
19	
20	typedef struct { TermRef ref; int after_comma; } PrintFrame;
21	#define PRINT_STACK_CAP 512U
22	
23	int term_write(TermRef ref, const TermArena *arena,
24	               const VarRegistry *vars, const AtomTable *atoms)
25	{
26	    PrintFrame  stack[PRINT_STACK_CAP];
27	    uint32_t    top = 0U;
28	    uint32_t    steps;
29	
30	    if (arena == NULL || vars == NULL || atoms == NULL) { return -1; }
31	
32	    stack[top].ref         = ref;
33	    stack[top].after_comma = 0;
34	    top++;
35	
36	    for (steps = 0U; steps < PRINT_STACK_CAP && top > 0U; ++steps) {
37	        PrintFrame   frame = stack[--top];
38	        TermRef      dr    = deref(frame.ref, arena, vars);
39	        const Term  *t     = term_get(arena, dr);
40	
41	        if (t == NULL) { printf("_"); continue; }
42	
43	        switch (t->kind) {
44	            case TERM_KIND_ATOM: {
45	                const char *sp = atom_spelling(atoms, t->data.atom_id);
46	                printf("%s", sp ? sp : "_");
47	                break;
48	            }
49	            case TERM_KIND_INT:
50	                printf("%d", (int)t->data.int_val);
51	                break;
52	            case TERM_KIND_VAR: {
53	                WalkResult wr = var_walk(vars, t->data.var.var_id);
54	                if (wr.tag == TERM_UNBOUND) {
55	                    /* print variable name */
56	                    const char *vn = t->data.var.name;
57	                    printf("%s", (vn && vn[0]) ? vn : "_");
58	                } else if (wr.tag == TERM_ATOM) {
59	                    const char *sp = atom_spelling(atoms, wr.value.atom_id);
60	                    printf("%s", sp ? sp : "_");
61	                } else if (wr.tag == TERM_TERMREF) {
62	                    /* recurse: push the bound TermRef onto print stack */
63	                    if (top < PRINT_STACK_CAP) {
64	                        stack[top].ref         = wr.value.term_ref;
65	                        stack[top].after_comma = 0;
66	                        top++;
67	                    }
68	                } else {
69	                    printf("_");
70	                }
71	                break;
72	            }
73	            case TERM_KIND_COMPOUND: {
74	                uint8_t arity = t->data.compound.arity;
75	                const char *fn = atom_spelling(atoms, t->data.compound.functor);
76	                printf("%s(", fn ? fn : "?");
77	                if (top + (uint32_t)arity >= PRINT_STACK_CAP) {
78	                    printf("...)");
79	                    break;
80	                }
81	                /* push closing paren sentinel as atom ')', then args reversed */
82	                {
83	                    AtomId rparen = atom_lookup(atoms, ")sentinel_rparen");
84	                    (void)rparen;
85	                    /* simpler: print recursively bounded by arity */
86	                    uint8_t i;
87	                    for (i = 0U; i < arity; ++i) {
88	                        TermRef arg = term_arg(arena, dr, i);
89	                        if (i > 0U) { printf(","); }
90	                        term_write(arg, arena, vars, atoms);
91	                    }
92	                }
93	                printf(")");
94	                break;
95	            }
96	        }
97	    }
98	    return 0;
99	}

105	static BuiltinResult bi_true(TermRef goal, SolverEnv *env)
106	{
107	    (void)goal; (void)env;
108	    return BUILTIN_SUCCEED;
109	}
110	
111	static BuiltinResult bi_fail(TermRef goal, SolverEnv *env)
112	{
113	    (void)goal; (void)env;
114	    return BUILTIN_FAIL;
115	}
116	
117	static BuiltinResult bi_cut(TermRef goal, SolverEnv *env)
118	{
119	    (void)goal; (void)env;
120	    return BUILTIN_CUT;
121	}
122	
123	static BuiltinResult bi_unify(TermRef goal, SolverEnv *env)
124	{
125	    TermRef a, b;
126	    uint32_t mark;
127	
128	    a = term_arg(env->arena, goal, 0U);
129	    b = term_arg(env->arena, goal, 1U);
130	    if (a == TERM_REF_INVALID || b == TERM_REF_INVALID) { return BUILTIN_FAIL; }
131	
132	    mark = trail_mark(&env->trail);
133	    if (unify(a, b, env->arena, env->vars, &env->trail)) {
134	        return BUILTIN_SUCCEED;
135	    }
136	    trail_undo(&env->trail, env->vars, mark);
137	    return BUILTIN_FAIL;
138	}
139	
140	static BuiltinResult bi_not_unify(TermRef goal, SolverEnv *env)
141	{
142	    TermRef  a, b;
143	    uint32_t mark;
144	    int      ok;
145	
146	    a = term_arg(env->arena, goal, 0U);
147	    b = term_arg(env->arena, goal, 1U);
148	    if (a == TERM_REF_INVALID || b == TERM_REF_INVALID) { return BUILTIN_FAIL; }
149	
150	    mark = trail_mark(&env->trail);
151	    ok   = unify(a, b, env->arena, env->vars, &env->trail);
152	    trail_undo(&env->trail, env->vars, mark);
153	    return ok ? BUILTIN_FAIL : BUILTIN_SUCCEED;
154	}
155	
156	static BuiltinResult bi_write(TermRef goal, SolverEnv *env)
157	{
158	    TermRef arg = term_arg(env->arena, goal, 0U);
159	    if (arg == TERM_REF_INVALID) { return BUILTIN_FAIL; }
160	    term_write(arg, env->arena, env->vars, env->atoms);
161	    return BUILTIN_SUCCEED;
162	}
163	
164	static BuiltinResult bi_nl(TermRef goal, SolverEnv *env)
165	{
166	    (void)goal; (void)env;
167	    printf("\n");
168	    return BUILTIN_SUCCEED;
169	}
170	
171	static BuiltinResult bi_assert(TermRef goal, SolverEnv *env)
172	{
173	    TermRef     arg = term_arg(env->arena, goal, 0U);
174	    ParsedClause pc;
175	    uint32_t     i;
176	
177	    if (arg == TERM_REF_INVALID) { return BUILTIN_FAIL; }
178	
179	    memset(&pc, 0, sizeof(ParsedClause));
180	
181	    /* arg is either a head atom/compound, or ':-'(Head, Body) */
182	    {
183	        const Term *t = term_get(env->arena, deref(arg, env->arena, env->vars));
184	        if (t == NULL) { return BUILTIN_FAIL; }
185	
186	        if (t->kind == TERM_KIND_COMPOUND &&
187	            t->data.compound.arity == 2U) {
188	            /* check functor == ':-' */
189	            const char *fn = atom_spelling(env->atoms,
190	                                           t->data.compound.functor);
191	            if (fn != NULL && strncmp(fn, ":-", 3) == 0) {
192	                pc.head = term_arg(env->arena, arg, 0U);
193	                /* body: single goal or conjunction */
194	                TermRef body_ref = term_arg(env->arena, arg, 1U);
195	                pc.body[0]  = body_ref;
196	                pc.body_len = 1U;
197	                /* TODO: flatten conjunctions (','(A,B)) */
198	                goto do_assert;
199	            }
200	        }
201	        pc.head     = arg;
202	        pc.body_len = 0U;
203	    }
204	
205	    do_assert:
206	    (void)i;
207	    if (db_assert_last(env->db, &pc, env->arena) < 0) { return BUILTIN_FAIL; }
208	    return BUILTIN_SUCCEED;
209	}
210	
211	static BuiltinResult bi_retract(TermRef goal, SolverEnv *env)
212	{
213	    TermRef     arg = term_arg(env->arena, goal, 0U);
214	    const Term *t;
215	    AtomId      functor;
216	    uint8_t     arity;
217	
218	    if (arg == TERM_REF_INVALID) { return BUILTIN_FAIL; }
219	
220	    t = term_get(env->arena, deref(arg, env->arena, env->vars));
221	    if (t == NULL) { return BUILTIN_FAIL; }
222	
223	    if (t->kind == TERM_KIND_ATOM) {
224	        functor = t->data.atom_id;
225	        arity   = 0U;
226	    } else if (t->kind == TERM_KIND_COMPOUND) {
227	        functor = t->data.compound.functor;
228	        arity   = t->data.compound.arity;
229	    } else {
230	        return BUILTIN_FAIL;
231	    }
232	
233	    if (db_retract_first(env->db, functor, arity) < 0) { return BUILTIN_FAIL; }
234	    return BUILTIN_SUCCEED;
235	}

237	/* -------------------------------------------------------------------------
238	 * Dispatch table
239	 * ------------------------------------------------------------------------- */
240	
241	typedef struct {
242	    const char *name;
243	    uint8_t     arity;
244	    BuiltinFn   fn;
245	} BuiltinEntry;
246	
247	static const BuiltinEntry BUILTIN_TABLE[] = {
248	    { "true",   0U, bi_true       },
249	    { "fail",   0U, bi_fail       },
250	    { "false",  0U, bi_fail       },
251	    { "!",      0U, bi_cut        },
252	    { "=",      2U, bi_unify      },
253	    { "\\=",    2U, bi_not_unify  },
254	    { "write",  1U, bi_write      },
255	    { "nl",     0U, bi_nl         },
256	    { "assert", 1U, bi_assert     },
257	    { "assertz",1U, bi_assert     },
258	    { "retract",1U, bi_retract    }
259	};
260	
261	#define BUILTIN_TABLE_SIZE \
262	    ((uint32_t)(sizeof(BUILTIN_TABLE) / sizeof(BUILTIN_TABLE[0])))
263	
264	BuiltinFn builtin_lookup(AtomId functor, uint8_t arity, const AtomTable *atoms)
265	{
266	    const char *name;
267	    uint32_t    i;
268	
269	    if (functor == ATOM_ID_INVALID || atoms == NULL) { return NULL; }
270	
271	    name = atom_spelling(atoms, functor);
272	    if (name == NULL) { return NULL; }
273	
274	    for (i = 0U; i < BUILTIN_TABLE_SIZE; ++i) {
275	        if (BUILTIN_TABLE[i].arity == arity &&
276	            strncmp(BUILTIN_TABLE[i].name, name, ATOM_MAX_LEN) == 0) {
277	            return BUILTIN_TABLE[i].fn;
278	        }
279	    }
280	    return NULL;
281	}

[728] [tool_result]  RESULT: 1	/*
2	 * solver.c — Backtracking SLD resolution engine.
3	 *
4	 * Execution is iterative over an explicit goal stack and choice-point stack.
5	 * No C-stack recursion (JPL Rule 1). All stacks are bounded (JPL Rule 2).
6	 *
7	 * Algorithm per iteration:
8	 *   1. Pop the top goal.
9	 *   2. Deref it to find functor/arity.
10	 *   3. If it is a built-in, dispatch and handle result.
11	 *   4. Otherwise, look up matching clauses in the database.
12	 *   5. If multiple clauses match, push a ChoicePoint for all but the first.
13	 *   6. Unify goal with clause head; push body goals (reversed) onto goal stack.
14	 *   7. On failure, pop the most recent ChoicePoint and restore state.
15	 *
16	 * Variable scoping: each clause application must use fresh variables.
17	 * We achieve this by copying clause terms into a per-application TermArena
18	 * region and renaming variables — implemented as a "variant" step that
19	 * allocates new VarIds for each clause body instantiation.
20	 */
21	
22	#include "../include/solver.h"
23	#include "../include/builtins.h"
24	#include "../include/log.h"
25	#include <string.h>
26	#include <assert.h>
27	
28	/* -------------------------------------------------------------------------
29	 * Internal frame types
30	 * ------------------------------------------------------------------------- */
31	
32	typedef struct {
33	    TermRef goals[GOAL_STACK_CAPACITY];
34	    uint32_t top;
35	} GoalStack;
36	
37	typedef struct {
38	    uint32_t clause_idx;   /* next clause index to try in db->clauses[] */
39	    uint32_t clause_end;   /* one past the last clause for this pred     */
40	    TermRef  goal;         /* the goal that created this choice point     */
41	    uint32_t trail_mark;   /* trail top before this choice point          */
42	    uint32_t goal_top;     /* goal stack top before this choice point     */
43	} ChoicePoint;
44	
45	typedef struct {
46	    ChoicePoint items[CHOICE_STACK_CAPACITY];
47	    uint32_t    top;
48	} ChoiceStack;
49	
50	/* -------------------------------------------------------------------------
51	 * Variable renaming (clause instantiation)
52	 *
53	 * When a clause is selected, its variables must be renamed to fresh ones so
54	 * different invocations don't share bindings.  We copy each variable in the
55	 * clause body/head to a new VarId in env->vars, building a rename map.
56	 *
57	 * Rename map: old VarId → new VarId, stored in a flat parallel array.
58	 * Bounded by VAR_REGISTRY_CAPACITY.
59	 * ------------------------------------------------------------------------- */
60	
61	#define RENAME_MAP_CAP VAR_REGISTRY_CAPACITY
62	
63	typedef struct {
64	    VarId old_id[RENAME_MAP_CAP];
65	    VarId new_id[RENAME_MAP_CAP];
66	    uint32_t count;
67	} RenameMap;
68	
69	static VarId rename_lookup(const RenameMap *rm, VarId old)
70	{
71	    uint32_t i;
72	    for (i = 0U; i < rm->count; ++i) {
73	        if (rm->old_id[i] == old) { return rm->new_id[i]; }
74	    }
75	    return VAR_ID_INVALID;
76	}
77	
78	/* rename_term — produce a copy of ref with all variables renamed.
79	 * Returns TERM_REF_INVALID on error. Iterative via small recursion-free
80	 * helper that processes one term at a time (arity bounded by TERM_MAX_ARITY). */
81	static TermRef rename_term(TermRef ref, const TermArena *arena,
82	                            SolverEnv *env, RenameMap *rm,
83	                            const AtomTable *atoms)
84	{
85	    const Term *t;
86	    uint32_t    step;
87	
88	    (void)atoms;
89	
90	    /* Iterative: we handle the common non-compound cases directly.
91	     * For compounds, we rename args with a bounded loop (arity ≤ TERM_MAX_ARITY). */
92	    for (step = 0U; step < TERM_ARENA_CAPACITY; ++step) {
93	        t = term_get(arena, ref);
94	        if (t == NULL) { return TERM_REF_INVALID; }
95	
96	        switch (t->kind) {
97	            case TERM_KIND_ATOM:
98	                return term_make_atom(env->arena, t->data.atom_id);
99	
100	            case TERM_KIND_INT:
101	                return term_make_int(env->arena, t->data.int_val);
102	
103	            case TERM_KIND_VAR: {
104	                VarId old = t->data.var.var_id;
105	                VarId fresh = rename_lookup(rm, old);
106	                if (fresh == VAR_ID_INVALID) {
107	                    /* name is stored directly in the Term node (clause_arena) */
108	                    const char *vname = t->data.var.name;
109	                    fresh = var_alloc(env->vars, vname[0] ? vname : "_");
110	                    if (fresh == VAR_ID_INVALID) { return TERM_REF_INVALID; }
111	                    if (rm->count < RENAME_MAP_CAP) {
112	                        rm->old_id[rm->count] = old;
113	                        rm->new_id[rm->count] = fresh;
114	                        rm->count++;
115	                    }
116	                }
117	                return term_make_var(env->arena, fresh, t->data.var.name);
118	            }
119	
120	            case TERM_KIND_COMPOUND: {
121	                TermRef  new_args[TERM_MAX_ARITY];
122	                uint8_t  arity = t->data.compound.arity;
123	                uint8_t  i;
124	                for (i = 0U; i < arity; ++i) {
125	                    TermRef arg = term_arg(arena, ref, i);
126	                    new_args[i] = rename_term(arg, arena, env, rm, atoms);
127	                    if (new_args[i] == TERM_REF_INVALID) { return TERM_REF_INVALID; }
128	                }
129	                return term_make_compound(env->arena, t->data.compound.functor,
130	                                          arity, new_args);
131	            }
132	
133	            default:
134	                return TERM_REF_INVALID;
135	        }
136	        break; /* each case returns — this is unreachable but satisfies Rule 2 */
137	    }
138	    return TERM_REF_INVALID;
139	}
140	
141	/* -------------------------------------------------------------------------
142	 * GoalStack helpers
143	 * ------------------------------------------------------------------------- */
144	
145	static int gs_push(GoalStack *gs, TermRef g)
146	{
147	    if (gs->top >= GOAL_STACK_CAPACITY) { return -1; }
148	    gs->goals[gs->top++] = g;
149	    return 0;
150	}
151	
152	static TermRef gs_pop(GoalStack *gs)
153	{
154	    if (gs->top == 0U) { return TERM_REF_INVALID; }
155	    return gs->goals[--gs->top];
156	}
157	
158	/* -------------------------------------------------------------------------
159	 * Public API
160	 * ------------------------------------------------------------------------- */
161	
162	int solver_env_init(SolverEnv *env, TermArena *arena, TermArena *clause_arena,
163	                    VarRegistry *vars, VarRegistry *clause_vars,
164	                    AtomTable *atoms, Database *db)
165	{
166	    if (env == NULL || arena == NULL || clause_arena == NULL ||
167	        vars == NULL || clause_vars == NULL ||
168	        atoms == NULL || db == NULL) { return -1; }
169	    memset(env, 0, sizeof(SolverEnv));
170	    env->arena        = arena;
171	    env->clause_arena = clause_arena;
172	    env->vars         = vars;
173	    env->clause_vars  = clause_vars;
174	    env->atoms        = atoms;
175	    env->db           = db;
176	    trail_init(&env->trail);
177	    return 0;
178	}
179	
180	uint32_t solve(SolverEnv *env, const TermRef *goals, uint32_t goal_count,
181	               SolutionCallback cb, void *userdata)
182	{
183	    GoalStack   gs;
184	    ChoiceStack cs;
185	    uint32_t    solutions = 0U;
186	    uint32_t    gi;
187	    uint32_t    iter;
188	
189	    if (env == NULL || goals == NULL || goal_count == 0U || cb == NULL) {
190	        return 0U;
191	    }
192	
193	    memset(&gs, 0, sizeof(GoalStack));
194	    memset(&cs, 0, sizeof(ChoiceStack));
195	
196	    /* push initial goals in reverse order (top of stack = first goal) */
197	    for (gi = goal_count; gi > 0U; --gi) {
198	        if (gs_push(&gs, goals[gi - 1U]) < 0) { return 0U; }
199	    }
200	
201	    for (iter = 0U; iter < (uint32_t)1e7; ++iter) {
202	
203	        /* ---- SUCCESS: no goals remain ---------------------------------- */
204	        if (gs.top == 0U) {
205	            solutions++;
206	            SolveSignal sig = cb(env, goals, goal_count, userdata);
207	            if (sig == SOLVE_STOP) { return solutions; }
208	
209	            /* backtrack to last choice point */
210	            if (cs.top == 0U) { return solutions; }
211	            goto backtrack;
212	        }
213	
214	        /* ---- pop next goal -------------------------------------------- */
215	        {
216	            TermRef     goal    = gs_pop(&gs);
217	            TermRef     dgoal   = deref(goal, env->arena, env->vars);
218	            const Term *gt      = term_get(env->arena, dgoal);
219	            AtomId      functor = ATOM_ID_INVALID;
220	            uint8_t     arity   = 0U;
221	
222	            if (gt == NULL) { goto backtrack; }
223	
224	            if (gt->kind == TERM_KIND_ATOM) {
225	                functor = gt->data.atom_id;
226	                arity   = 0U;
227	            } else if (gt->kind == TERM_KIND_COMPOUND) {
228	                functor = gt->data.compound.functor;
229	                arity   = gt->data.compound.arity;
230	            } else {
231	                goto backtrack; /* non-callable */
232	            }
233	
234	            /* ---- built-in? -------------------------------------------- */
235	            {
236	                BuiltinFn bf = builtin_lookup(functor, arity, env->atoms);
237	                if (bf != NULL) {
238	                    BuiltinResult br = bf(dgoal, env);
239	                    switch (br) {
240	                        case BUILTIN_SUCCEED: continue;
241	                        case BUILTIN_FAIL:    goto backtrack;
242	                        case BUILTIN_CUT:
243	                            /* discard choice points up to last cut barrier */
244	                            /* (simplified: discard all for now) */
245	                            cs.top = 0U;
246	                            continue;
247	                        case BUILTIN_ERROR:   return solutions;
248	                    }
249	                }
250	            }
251	
252	            /* ---- user-defined clause lookup --------------------------- */
253	            {
254	                const PredEntry *pred = db_get_pred(env->db, functor, arity);
255	                VLOG_SUBSYS("SOLVE", "goal functor=%u arity=%u pred=%s count=%u",
256	                            functor, arity,
257	                            pred ? atom_spelling(env->atoms, functor) : "?NONE",
258	                            pred ? pred->count : 0U);
259	                if (pred == NULL || pred->count == 0U) {
260	                    VLOG_SUBSYS("SOLVE", "%s", "no clauses -> backtrack");
261	                    goto backtrack;
262	                }
263	
264	                /* push choice point if more than one clause */
265	                if (pred->count > 1U && cs.top < CHOICE_STACK_CAPACITY) {
266	                    ChoicePoint *cp = &cs.items[cs.top++];
267	                    cp->clause_idx  = pred->first + 1U; /* skip first */
268	                    cp->clause_end  = pred->first + pred->count;
269	                    cp->goal        = goal;
270	                    cp->trail_mark  = trail_mark(&env->trail);
271	                    cp->goal_top    = gs.top;
272	                }
273	
274	                /* try first clause */
275	                {
276	                    const StoredClause *sc = db_get_clause(env->db, pred->first);
277	                    RenameMap rm;
278	                    TermRef   fresh_head;
279	                    uint32_t  mark;
280	                    uint32_t  bi;
281	
282	                    if (sc == NULL) { goto backtrack; }
283	
284	                    memset(&rm, 0, sizeof(RenameMap));
285	                    mark       = trail_mark(&env->trail);
286	                    fresh_head = rename_term(sc->head, env->clause_arena,
287	                                             env, &rm, env->atoms);
288	                    if (fresh_head == TERM_REF_INVALID) { goto backtrack; }
289	
290	                    VLOG_SUBSYS("SOLVE", "trying clause head ref=%u", sc->head);
291	                    if (!unify(dgoal, fresh_head, env->arena,
292	                               env->vars, &env->trail)) {
293	                        VLOG_SUBSYS("SOLVE", "%s", "unify failed -> backtrack");
294	                        trail_undo(&env->trail, env->vars, mark);
295	                        goto backtrack;
296	                    }
297	                    VLOG_SUBSYS("SOLVE", "unified ok, body_len=%u", sc->body_len);
298	
299	                    /* push body goals in reverse */
300	                    for (bi = sc->body_len; bi > 0U; --bi) {
301	                        TermRef fresh_body = rename_term(sc->body[bi - 1U],
302	                                                         env->clause_arena,
303	                                                         env, &rm, env->atoms);
304	                        if (fresh_body == TERM_REF_INVALID ||
305	                            gs_push(&gs, fresh_body) < 0) {
306	                            trail_undo(&env->trail, env->vars, mark);
307	                            goto backtrack;
308	                        }
309	                    }
310	                }
311	            }
312	            continue;
313	        }
314	
315	        backtrack:
316	        {
317	            if (cs.top == 0U) { return solutions; }
318	
319	            {
320	                ChoicePoint *cp = &cs.items[cs.top - 1U];
321	
322	                /* restore trail and goal stack */
323	                trail_undo(&env->trail, env->vars, cp->trail_mark);
324	                gs.top = cp->goal_top;
325	
326	                if (cp->clause_idx >= cp->clause_end) {
327	                    cs.top--;
328	                    goto backtrack;
329	                }
330	
331	                /* try next clause from this choice point */
332	                {
333	                    const StoredClause *sc =
334	                        db_get_clause(env->db, cp->clause_idx);
335	                    TermRef  dgoal = deref(cp->goal, env->arena, env->vars);
336	                    RenameMap rm;
337	                    TermRef   fresh_head;
338	                    uint32_t  mark;
339	                    uint32_t  bi;
340	
341	                    cp->clause_idx++;
342	                    if (cp->clause_idx >= cp->clause_end) { cs.top--; }
343	
344	                    if (sc == NULL) { goto backtrack; }
345	
346	                    memset(&rm, 0, sizeof(RenameMap));
347	                    mark       = trail_mark(&env->trail);
348	                    fresh_head = rename_term(sc->head, env->clause_arena,
349	                                             env, &rm, env->atoms);
350	                    if (fresh_head == TERM_REF_INVALID) { goto backtrack; }
351	
352	                    if (!unify(dgoal, fresh_head, env->arena,
353	                               env->vars, &env->trail)) {
354	                        trail_undo(&env->trail, env->vars, mark);
355	                        goto backtrack;
356	                    }
357	
358	                    for (bi = sc->body_len; bi > 0U; --bi) {
359	                        TermRef fresh_body = rename_term(sc->body[bi - 1U],
360	                                                         env->clause_arena,
361	                                                         env, &rm, env->atoms);
362	                        if (fresh_body == TERM_REF_INVALID ||
363	                            gs_push(&gs, fresh_body) < 0) {
364	                            trail_undo(&env->trail, env->vars, mark);
365	                            goto backtrack;
366	                        }
367	                    }
368	                }
369	            }
370	            continue;
371	        }
372	    }
373	
374	    return solutions;
375	}
